import os
from Bio.PDB import PDBParser, PDBIO, Select

class CleanSelect(Select):
    def __init__(self, keep_metals=True, keep_ligand=None, keep_chains=None):
        self.keep_metals = keep_metals
        self.keep_ligand = keep_ligand  # lista de nombres de ligandos a mantener
        self.keep_chains = keep_chains  # lista de cadenas a mantener (None = todas)

    def accept_residue(self, residue):
        # Mantener aminoácidos estándar
        if residue.get_resname() in ['ALA','ARG','ASN','ASP','CYS','GLN','GLU','GLY',
                                     'HIS','ILE','LEU','LYS','MET','PHE','PRO','SER',
                                     'THR','TRP','TYR','VAL','HIP','HID','HIE']:
            if self.keep_chains is None or residue.parent.id in self.keep_chains:
                return True
        # Mantener metales (CU, ZN) si está habilitado
        if self.keep_metals and residue.get_resname() in ['CU','ZN','MG','CA','MN','FE']:
            return True
        # Mantener ligandos específicos
        if self.keep_ligand and residue.get_resname() in self.keep_ligand:
            return True
        return False

    def accept_atom(self, atom):
        # Rechazar hidrógenos
        if atom.element == 'H' or atom.get_name()[0] == 'H':
            return False
        return True

# Configurar limpieza por estructura
configs = {
    '2Y9X': {
        'chains': ['A', 'B'],  # Solo tirosinasa, no lectina-like
        'ligands': ['TRL'],    # Tropolone (si existe)
        'metals': True
    },
    '3NQ1': {
        'chains': ['A', 'B'],
        'ligands': ['KOJ'],    # Ácido kójico
        'metals': True,
        'remove_extra_zn': True  # Quitar Zn de cristalización, solo dejar CU
    },
    '5I38': {
        'chains': ['A', (base) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr$
'B'],
        'ligands': ['KOJ'],
        'metals': True
    },
    '5M8L': {
        'chains': ['A', 'B'],  # TYRP1 humano
        'ligands': [],         # Apo (sin ligando)
        'metals': True
    },
    '4P6R': {
        'chains': ['A', 'B'],
        'ligands': ['TYR'],    # L-tirosina (sustrato)
        'metals': True
    }
}

parser = PDBParser(QUIET=True)

for pdb_id, config in configs.items():
    print(f"\n=== Limpiando {pdb_id} ===")
    structure = parser.get_structure(pdb_id, f'{pdb_id}.pdb')

# ¿Qué heteros quedaron en cada clean?
for f in *_clean.pdb; do
  echo "=== $f ==="
  grep "^HETATM" $f | awk '{print $4}' | sort | uniq -c
done

# 3NQ1: el sitio activo es CU (501/502); los ZN son de cristalización -> quitar TODOS los ZN
awk '!($1=="HETATM" && $4=="ZN")' 3NQ1_clean.pdb > tmp.pdb && mv tmp.pdb 3NQ1_clean.pdb

# 2Y9X y 5M8L: quitar metales de buffer (MG/CA/NA/CL) si aparecen; conservar CU / ZN del sitio activo
for f in 2Y9X_clean.pdb 5M8L_clean.pdb; do
  awk '!($1=="HETATM" && ($4=="MG" || $4=="CA" || $4=="NA" || $4=="CL"))' $f > tmp.pdb && mv tmp.pdb $f
done

# Re-verificar metales finales
for f in *_clean.pdb; do
  echo "=== $f ==="
  grep "^HETATM" $f | grep -E " CU | ZN " | awk '{print $4, $6}' | sort | uniq -c
done(base) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr$

python3 << 'PYEOF'
AA={'ALA','ARG','ASN','ASP','CYS','GLN','GLU','GLY','HIS','ILE','LEU','LYS',
    'MET','PHE','PRO','SER','THR','TRP','TYR','VAL'}
rules={
 '2Y9X': dict(chains={'A','B'}, metals={'CU'}, mresn=None,      lig={'TRL'}),
 '3NQ1': dict(chains={'A','B'}, metals={'CU'}, mresn=None,      lig={'KOJ'}),
 '5I38': dict(chains={'A','B'}, metals={'CU'}, mresn=None,      lig={'KOJ'}),
 '5M8L': dict(chains={'A','B'}, metals={'ZN'}, mresn={512,513}, lig=set()),
 '4P6R': dict(chains={'A','B'}, metals={'ZN'}, mresn=None,      lig={'TYR'}),
}
for pid,r in rules.items():
    inv={}; out=[]
    for line in open(f'{pid}.pdb'):
        if not line.startswith(('ATOM','HETATM')): continue
        rn=line[17:20].strip(); ch=line[21]; rs=line[22:26].strip()
        if line.startswith('ATOM'):
            if rn in AA and ch in r['chains']: out.append(line)
        else:
            inv[rn]=inv.get(rn,0)+1
            if rn in r['metals'] and ch in r['chains'] and (r['mresn'] is None or int(rs) in r['mresn']):
                out.append(line)
            elif rn in r['lig'] and ch in r['chains']:
                out.append(line)
    open(f'{pid}_clean.pdb','w').writelines(out)
    nmet=sum(1 for l in out if l[17:20].strip() in r['metals'])
    nlig=sum(1 for l in out if l[17:20].strip() in r['lig'])
    print(f"{pid} | heteros originales: {inv}")
    print(f"     -> protein+{nmet} metales+{nlig} ligando = {len(out)} lineas")

    n=0; out=open(f'{ID}_{ph}_receptor.pdbqt','w')
    for line in open(f'{ID}_{ph}.pqr'):
        if not line.startswith(('ATOM','HETATM')): continue
        p=line.split()
        if len(p)>=11: name,resn,chain,resseq=p[2],p[3],p[4],p[5]; x,y,z,q=map(float,p[6:10])
        elif len(p)>=10: name,resn,chain,resseq=p[2],p[3],'A',p[4]; x,y,z,q=map(float,p[5:9])
        else: continue
        n+=1; rsi=int(''.join(c for c in resseq if c.isdigit()) or 0)
        out.write(f"{line[0:6].strip():<6}{n:>5} {name:<4} {resn:>3} {chain:1}{rsi:>4}    {x:8.3f}{y:8.3f}{z:8.3f}  1.00  0.00    {q:+6.3f} {tipo(name,resn):<2}\n")
    for line in open(f'{ID}_het.pdb'):
        p=line.split(); name,resn,chain,resseq=p[2],p[3],p[4],p[5]
        x,y,z=float(p[6]),float(p[7]),float(p[8]); n+=1
        q=+2.0 if resn in ('CU','ZN') else 0.0
        out.write(f"HETATM{n:>5} {name:<4} {resn:>3} {chain:1}{int(resseq):>4}    {x:8.3f}{y:8.3f}{z:8.3f}  1.00  0.00  {q:+6.3f} {tipo(name,resn):<2}\n")
    out.write("TER\nEND\n"); out.close()
    print(f'{ID}_{ph}_receptor.pdbqt: {n} atomos')

AA={'ALA','ARG','ASN','ASP','CYS','GLN','GLU','GLY','HIS','ILE','LEU','LYS',
    'MET','PHE','PRO','SER','THR','TRP','TYR','VAL'}
rules={
 '2Y9X': dict(chains={'A','B'}, metals={'CU'}, mresn=None,      lig={'0TR','OTR','TRL'}),
 '3NQ1': dict(chains={'A','B'}, metals={'CU'}, mresn=None,      lig={'KOJ'}),
 '5I38': dict(chains={'A','B'}, metals={'CU'}, mresn=None,      lig={'KOJ'}),
 '5M8L': dict(chains={'A','C'}, metals={'ZN'}, mresn={512,513}, lig=set()),
 '4P6R': dict(chains={'A','B'}, metals={'ZN'}, mresn=None,      lig={'TYR'}),
}
for pid,r in rules.items():
    out=[]; nmet=0; nlig=0
    for line in open(f'{pid}.pdb'):
        if not line.startswith(('ATOM','HETATM')): continue
        rn=line[17:20].strip(); ch=line[21]; rs=line[22:26].strip()
        if line.startswith('ATOM'):
            if rn in AA and ch in r['chains']: out.append(line)
        else:
            if rn in r['metals'] and ch in r['chains'] and (r['mresn'] is None or int(rs) in r['mresn']):
                out.append(line); nmet+=1
            elif rn in r['lig'] and ch in r['chains']:
                out.append(line); nlig+=1
    open(f'{pid}_clean.pdb','w').writelines(out)
    print(f"{pid}: protein + {nmet} metales + {nlig} ligando = {len(out)} lineas")

python3 - "$ID" << 'PYEOF'
import sys
ID=sys.argv[1]
def tipo(name,resn):
    e=name[0]
    if e=='H': return 'HD'
    if e=='C': return 'A' if resn in ('PHE','TYR','TRP','HIS') and name not in ('CA','C','O') else 'C'
    if e=='N': return 'NA' if resn in ('ARG','LYS','HIS','HIP') else 'N'
    if e=='O': return 'OA'
    if e=='S': return 'SA'
    if resn in ('CU','ZN'): return resn
    return e
for ph in ('pH5','pH74'):
    n=0; out=open(f'{ID}_{ph}_receptor.pdbqt','w')
    for line in open(f'{ID}_{ph}.pqr'):
        if not line.startswith(('ATOM','HETATM')): continue
        p=line.split()
        if len(p)>=11: name,resn,chain,resseq=p[2],p[3],p[4],p[5]; x,y,z,q=map(float,p[6:10])
        elif len(p)>=10: name,resn,chain,resseq=p[2],p[3],'A',p[4]; x,y,z,q=map(float,p[5:9])
        else: continue
        n+=1; rsi=int(''.join(c for c in resseq if c.isdigit()) or 0)
        out.write(f"{line[0:6].strip():<6}{n:>5} {name:<4} {resn:>3} {chain:1}{rsi:>4}    {x:8.3f}{y:8.3f}{z:8.3f}  1.00  0.00    {q:+6.3f} {tipo(name,resn):<2}\n")
    for line in open(f'{ID}_het.pdb'):
        p=line.split(); name,resn,chain,resseq=p[2],p[3],p[4],p[5]
        x,y,z=float(p[6]),float(p[7]),float(p[8]); n+=1
        q=+2.0 if resn in ('CU','ZN') else 0.0
        out.write(f"HETATM{n:>5} {name:<4} {resn:>3} {chain:1}{int(resseq):>4}    {x:8.3f}{y:8.3f}{z:8.3f}  1.00  0.00  {q:+6.3f} {tipo(name,resn):<2}\n")
    out.write("TER\nEND\n"); out.close()
    print(f'{ID}_{ph}_receptor.pdbqt: {n} atomos')
PYEOF

import sys, numpy as np
ID=sys.argv[1]; xyz=[]
for line in open(f'{ID}_clean.pdb'):
    if line.startswith('ATOM'):
        xyz.append([float(line[30:38]),float(line[38:46]),float(line[46:54])])
xyz=np.array(xyz); c=xyz.mean(axis=0); size=(xyz.max(axis=0)-xyz.min(axis=0)+12).round(1)
print(f'caja {ID}: centro ({c[0]:.1f}, {c[1]:.1f}, {c[2]:.1f}) | size ({size[0]:.1f}, {size[1]:.1f}, {size[2]:.1f})')
open(f'{ID}_box.txt','w').write(f'{c[0]:.3f} {c[1]:.3f} {c[2]:.3f} {size[0]:.1f} {size[1]:.1f} {size[2]:.1f}\n')

import sys
ID=sys.argv[1]
def tipo(name,resn):
    e=name[0]
    if e=='H': return 'HD'

    if e=='N': return 'NA' if resn in ('ARG','LYS','HIS','HIP') else 'N'
    if e=='O': return 'OA'
    if e=='S': return 'SA'

    return e

!/bin/bash
ID=$1
PQR=/home/me/miniconda3/envs/pqr311/bin/pdb2pqr
cd /mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr

grep "^ATOM" ${ID}_clean.pdb > ${ID}_prot.pdb
printf "TER\nEND\n" >> ${ID}_prot.pdb
grep "^HETATM" ${ID}_clean.pdb > ${ID}_het.pdb

for PH in 5.0 7.4; do
  TAG=$(echo $PH | tr -d '.')
  $PQR --ff=AMBER --titration-state-method propka --with-ph $PH --whitespace \
       ${ID}_prot.pdb ${ID}_pH${TAG}.pqr > ${ID}_pH${TAG}.log 2>&1
  echo "-- ${ID} pH${PH}: HIP=$(grep -c HIP ${ID}_pH${TAG}.pqr) | neutras=$(grep -cE 'HID|HIE' ${ID}_pH${TAG}.pqr)"
done

python3 - "$ID" << 'PYEOF'
import sys
ID=sys.argv[1]
def tipo(name,resn):
    e=name[0]
    if e=='H': return 'HD'
    if e=='C': return 'A' if resn in ('PHE','TYR','TRP','HIS','HIP','HID','HIE') and name not in ('CA','C','O') else 'C'
    if e=='N': return 'NA' if resn in ('ARG','LYS','HIS','HIP','HID','HIE') else 'N'
    if e=='O': return 'OA'
    if e=='S': return 'SA'
    if resn in ('CU','ZN'): return resn
    return e
for TAG in ('50','74'):
    n=0; sumq=0.0
    out=open(f'{ID}_pH{TAG}_receptor.pdbqt','w')
    for line in open(f'{ID}_pH{TAG}.pqr'):
        if not line.startswith(('ATOM','HETATM')): continue
        p=line.split()
        if len(p)>=11:
            name,resn,chain,resseq=p[2],p[3],p[4],p[5]
            x,y,z,q=float(p[6]),float(p[7]),float(p[8]),float(p[9])
        elif len(p)>=10:
            name,resn,chain,resseq=p[2],p[3],'A',p[4]
            x,y,z,q=float(p[5]),float(p[6]),float(p[7]),float(p[8])
        else: continue
        n+=1; sumq+=q
        rsi=int(''.join(c for c in resseq if c.isdigit()) or 0)
        out.write(f"{line[0:6].strip():<6}{n:>5} {name:<4} {resn:>3} {chain:1}{rsi:>4}    {x:8.3f}{y:8.3f}{z:8.3f}  1.00  0.00    {q:+6.3f} {tipo(name,resn):<2}\n")
    for line in open(f'{ID}_het.pdb'):
        if not line.sta(pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr$
rtswith('HETATM'): continue
        name=line[12:16].strip(); resn=line[17:20].strip()
        ch=line[21] if line[21].strip() else 'A'
        rs=line[22:26].strip()
        x,y,z=float(line[30:38]),float(line[38:46]),float(line[46:54])
        n+=1
        q=+2.0 if resn in ('CU','ZN') else 0.0
        rsi=int(''.join(c for c in rs if c.isdigit()) or 0)
        out.write(f"HETATM{n:>5} {name:<4} {resn:>3} {ch:1}{rsi:>4}    {x:8.3f}{y:8.3f}{z:8.3f}  1.00  0.00    {q:+6.3f} {tipo(name,resn):<2}\n")
    out.write("TER\nEND\n"); out.close()

(pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr$ cd /mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr
rt sys, numpy as np
ID, TAG = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
CAT={'ARG','LYS','HIS'}; POL={'SER','THR','ASN','GLN','TYR','CYS'}
AA={'ALA','ARG','ASN','ASP','CYS','GLN','GLU','GLY','HIS','ILE','LEU','LYS',
    'MET','PHE','PRO','SER','THR','TRP','TYR','VAL'}

pose=[]; inm=False
for l in open(f'vina_{ID}_{TAG}_deca.pdbqt'):
    if l.startswith('MODEL 1'): inm=True; continue
    if l.startswith('ENDMDL') and inm: break
    if inm and l.startswith(('ATOM','HETATM')):
        pose.append([float(l[30:38]),float(l[38:46]),float(l[46:54])])
V=np.array(pose)

prot=[]
for l in open(f'{ID}_clean.pdb'):
    if l.startswith(('ATOM','HETATM')):
        prot.append((l[21],l[22:26].strip(),l[17:20].strip(),l[12:16].strip(),
                     [float(l[30:38]),float(l[38:46]),float(l[46:54])]))

hit={}; cu=[]; lig=[]
for ch,rs,rn,nm,xyz in prot:
    d=float(np.linalg.norm(V-np.array(xyz),axis=1).min())
    if rn in ('CU','ZN'): cu.append((rn,ch,rs,d))
    elif rn not in AA and rn!='HOH': lig.append((rn,ch,rs,d))
    if d<5.0 and rn in AA:
        k=(ch,rs,rn); hit[k]=min(hit.get(k,99),d)

print(f"=== {ID} {TAG} pose 1 | atomos deca: {len(V)} ===")
for (ch,rs,rn),d in sorted(hit.items(),key=lambda kv:kv[1])[:15]:
    t='CAT' if rn in CAT else 'pol' if rn in POL else 'hid'
    print(f"  {ch}{rs:>4} {rn:4s} {d:5.2f} {t}")
ncat=sum(1 for k in hit if k[2] in CAT)
print(f"  cationicos: {ncat}/{len(hit)} = {100*ncat/max(1,len(hit)):.1f}%")
allres={(ch,rs):rn for ch,rs,rn,nm,xyz in prot if rn in AA}
nb=sum(1 for rn in allres.values() if rn in CAT)
print(f"  baseline cationico: {nb}/{len(allres)} = {100*nb/len(allres):.1f}%")
print("  -- sitio binuclear:")
for rn,ch,rs,d in sorted(cu,key=lambda t:t[3])[:4]:
    print(f"     {rn} {ch}{rs}  {d:5.2f} A")
if lig:
    dmin=min(lig,key=lambda t:t[3])
    print(f"  -- ligando nativo: resn {dmin[0]} dmin {dmin[3]:.2f} A")
EOF

import sys, numpy as np
ID, TAG, POSE = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], int(sys.argv[3])
CAT={'ARG','LYS','HIS'}; POL={'SER','THR','ASN','GLN','TYR','CYS'}
AA={'ALA','ARG','ASN','ASP','CYS','GLN','GLU','GLY','HIS','ILE','LEU','LYS',
    'MET','PHE','PRO','SER','THR','TRP','TYR','VAL'}

pose=[]; inm=False; cur_pose=0
for l in open(f'vina_{ID}_{TAG}_deca.pdbqt'):
    if l.startswith('MODEL'):
        cur_pose += 1
        if cur_pose == POSE: inm = True
        continue
    if l.startswith('ENDMDL') and inm: break
    if inm and l.startswith(('ATOM','HETATM')):
        pose.append([float(l[30:38]),float(l[38:46]),float(l[46:54])])
V=np.array(pose)

prot=[]
for l in open(f'{ID}_clean.pdb'):
    if l.startswith(('ATOM','HETATM')):
        prot.append((l[21],l[22:26].strip(),l[17:20].strip(),l[12:16].strip(),
                     [float(l[30:38]),float(l[38:46]),float(l[46:54])]))

hit={}; cu=[]; lig=[]
for ch,rs,rn,nm,xyz in prot:
    d=float(np.linalg.norm(V-np.array(xyz),axis=1).min())
    if rn in ('CU','ZN'): cu.append((rn,ch,rs,d))
    elif rn not in AA and rn!='HOH': lig.append((rn,ch,rs,d))
    if d<5.0 and rn in AA:
        k=(ch,rs,rn); hit[k]=min(hit.get(k,99),d)

print(f"\n=== {ID} {TAG} pose {POSE} | atomos deca: {len(V)} ===")
for (ch,rs,rn),d in sorted(hit.items(),key=lambda kv:kv[1])[:12]:
    t='CAT' if rn in CAT else 'pol' if rn in POL else 'hid'
    print(f"  {ch}{rs:>4} {rn:4s} {d:5.2f} {t}")
ncat=sum(1 for k in hit if k[2] in CAT)
print(f"  cationicos: {ncat}/{len(hit)} = {100*ncat/max(1,len(hit)):.1f}%")
print("  -- sitio binuclear:")
for rn,ch,rs,d in sorted(cu,key=lambda t:t[3])[:4]:
    print(f"     {rn} {ch}{rs}  {d:5.2f} A")
if lig:
    dmin=min(lig,key=lambda t:t[3])
    print(f"  -- ligando nativo: resn {dmin[0]} dmin {dmin[3]:.2f} A")

(pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr$ cat > anal_tyr_pose.py << 'EOF'
> import sys, numpy as np
> ID, TAG, POSE = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], int(sys.argv[3])
> CAT={'ARG','LYS','HIS'}; POL={'SER','THR','ASN','GLN','TYR','CYS'}
> AA={'ALA','ARG','ASN','ASP','CYS','GLN','GLU','GLY','HIS','ILE','LEU','LYS',
>     'MET','PHE','PRO','SER','THR','TRP','TYR','VAL'}
>
> pose=[]; inm=False; cur_pose=0
> for l in open(f'vina_{ID}_{TAG}_deca.pdbqt'):
>     if l.startswith('MODEL'):
>         cur_pose += 1
>         if cur_pose == POSE: inm = True
>         continue
>     if l.startswith('ENDMDL') and inm: break
>     if inm and l.startswith(('ATOM','HETATM')):
>         pose.append([float(l[30:38]),float(l[38:46]),float(l[46:54])])
> V=np.array(pose)
>
> prot=[]
> for l in open(f'{ID}_clean.pdb'):
>     if l.startswith(('ATOM','HETATM')):
>         prot.append((l[21],l[22:26].strip(),l[17:20].strip(),l[12:16].strip(),
>                      [float(l[30:38]),float(l[38:46]),float(l[46:54])]))
>
> hit={}; cu=[]; lig=[]
> for ch,rs,rn,nm,xyz in prot:
>     d=float(np.linalg.norm(V-np.array(xyz),axis=1).min())
>     if rn in ('CU','ZN'): cu.append((rn,ch,rs,d))
>     elif rn not in AA and rn!='HOH': lig.append((rn,ch,rs,d))
>     if d<5.0 and rn in AA:
>         k=(ch,rs,rn); hit[k]=min(hit.get(k,99),d)
>
> print(f"\n=== {ID} {TAG} pose {POSE} | atomos deca: {len(V)} ===")
> for (ch,rs,rn),d in sorted(hit.items(),key=lambda kv:kv[1])[:12]:
>     t='CAT' if rn in CAT else 'pol' if rn in POL else 'hid'
>     print(f"  {ch}{rs:>4} {rn:4s} {d:5.2f} {t}")
> ncat=sum(1 for k in hit if k[2] in CAT)
> print(f"  cationicos: {ncat}/{len(hit)} = {100*ncat/max(1,len(hit)):.1f}%")
> print("  -- sitio binuclear:")
> for rn,ch,rs,d in sorted(cu,key=lambda t:t[3])[:4]:
>     print(f"     {rn} {ch}{rs}  {d:5.2f} A")
> if lig:
>     dmin=min(lig,key=lambda t:t[3])
>     print(f"  -- ligando nativo: resn {dmin[0]} dmin {dmin[3]:.2f} A")
> EOF

python3 << 'EOF'
def parse_pqr(filename):
    residues = {}
    with open(filename) as f:
        for line in f:
            if line.startswith('ATOM'):
                parts = line.split()
                if len(parts) >= 6:
                    resname = parts[3]
                    chain = parts[4]
                    resnum = parts[5]
                    key = f"{chain}{resnum}"
                    residues[key] = resname
    return residues

ph50 = parse_pqr('2Y9X_pH50.pqr')
ph74 = parse_pqr('2Y9X_pH74.pqr')

# Buscar diferencias
diffs = []
for key in ph50:
    if key in ph74:
        if ph50[key] != ph74[key]:
            diffs.append((key, ph50[key], ph74[key]))

print(f"Residuos que cambiaron entre pH 5.0 y 7.4: {len(diffs)}")
print("\nPrimeros 20 cambios:")
for key, r50, r74 in sorted(diffs)[:20]:
    print(f"  {key}: {r50} (pH5) -> {r74} (pH74)")

# Contar por tipo de cambio
changes = {}
for key, r50, r74 in diffs:
    change = f"{r50}->{r74}"
    changes[change] = changes.get(change, 0) + 1

print("\nResumen de cambios:")
for change, count in sorted(changes.(pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr$
items(), key=lambda x: -x[1]):
    print(f"  {change}: {count}")

na/bin/vina

for ID in 3NQ1 5I38 5M8L 4P6R; do
  for TAG in pH50 pH74; do
    echo "=== BLIND $ID $TAG ==="
    read CX CY CZ SX SY SZ < ${ID}_box.txt
    $VINA --receptor ${ID}_${TAG}_receptor.pdbqt \
         --ligand ../deca_alone.pdbqt \
         --center_x $CX --center_y $CY --center_z $CZ \
         --size_x $SX --size_y $SY --size_z $SZ \
         --exhaustiveness 8 --num_modes 20 \
         --out vina_${ID}_${TAG}_deca.pdbqt
    echo "-- pose 1:"
    grep -A 2 "mode |" vina_${ID}_${TAG}_deca.pdbqt | head -3
  done
done(pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr$ VINA=/home/me/miniconda3/envs/vina/bin/vina

# Relanzar los blinds fallidos
VINA=/home/me/miniconda3/envs/vina/bin/vina

for ID in 5M8L 4P6R; do
  for TAG in pH50 pH74; do
    echo "=== BLIND $ID $TAG (sin Zn) ==="
    read CX CY CZ SX SY SZ < ${ID}_box.txt
    $VINA --receptor ${ID}_${TAG}_receptor_noZn.pdbqt \
         --ligand ../deca_alone.pdbqt \
         --center_x $CX --center_y $CY --center_z $CZ \
         --size_x $SX --size_y $SY --size_z $SZ \
         --exhaustiveness 8 --num_modes 20 \
         --out vina_${ID}_${TAG}_deca.pdbqt
    grep -A 2 "mode |" vina_${ID}_${TAG}_deca.pdbqt | head -3
  done
done(pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr$

(pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr$ cd /mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr
,'HIS'}
POL={'SER','THR','ASN','GLN','TYR','CYS'}
AA={'ALA','ARG','ASN','ASP','CYS','GLN','GLU','GLY','HIS','ILE','LEU','LYS',
    'MET','PHE','PRO','SER','THR','TRP','TYR','VAL'}

def analizar(ID, TAG):
    pose=[]; inm=False
    pfile = Path(f'vina_{ID}_{TAG}_deca.pdbqt')
    if not pfile.exists():
        return None
    for l in open(pfile):
        if l.startswith('MODEL 1'): inm=True; continue
        if l.startswith('ENDMDL') and inm: break
        if inm and l.startswith(('ATOM','HETATM')):
            pose.append([float(l[30:38]),float(l[38:46]),float(l[46:54])])
    if not pose:
        return None
    V=np.array(pose)

    prot=[]
    for l in open(f'{ID}_clean.pdb'):
        if l.startswith(('ATOM','HETATM')):
            prot.append((l[21],l[22:26].strip(),l[17:20].strip(),l[12:16].strip(),
                         [float(l[30:38]),float(l[38:46]),float(l[46:54])]))

    hit={}; cu=[]; lig=[]
    for ch,rs,rn,nm,xyz in prot:
        d=float(np.linalg.norm(V-np.array(xyz),axis=1).min())
        if rn in ('CU','ZN'): cu.append((rn,ch,rs,d))
        elif rn not in AA and rn!='HOH': lig.append((rn,ch,rs,d))
        if d<5.0 and rn in AA:
            k=(ch,rs,rn); hit[k]=min(hit.get(k,99),d)

    ncat=sum(1 for k in hit if k[2] in CAT)
    pct_cat=100*ncat/max(1,len(hit))

    allres={(ch,rs):rn for ch,rs,rn,nm,xyz in prot if rn in AA}
    nb=sum(1 for rn in allres.values() if rn in CAT)
    baseline=100*nb/len(allres)

    dcu=min([t[3] for t in cu]) if cu else 999
    dlig=min([t[3] for t in lig]) if lig else 999

    return {
        'hit': len(hit),
        'ncat': ncat,
        'pct_cat': pct_cat,
        'baseline': baseline,
        'enrich': pct_cat/baseline if baseline>0 else 0,
        'dcu': dcu,
        'dlig': dlig,
        'top3': [(k[0],k[1],k[2]) for k,d in sorted(hit.items(),key=lambda kv:kv[1])[:3]]
    }

print("=== Análisis comparativo de sitios de unión ===")

import os
from pathlib import Path

# Extraer pose 1 de cada docking y convertir a PDB
dockings = [
    ('2Y9X', 'pH50'), ('2Y9X', 'pH74'),
    ('3NQ1', 'pH50'), ('3NQ1', 'pH74'),
    ('5I38', 'pH50'), ('5I38', 'pH74'),
    ('5M8L', 'pH50'), ('5M8L', 'pH74'),
    ('4P6R', 'pH50'), ('4P6R', 'pH74')
]

for ID, TAG in dockings:
    vina_file = f'vina_{ID}_{TAG}_deca.pdbqt'
    if not Path(vina_file).exists():
        print(f"⚠️  {vina_file} no existe")
        continue

    pose_file = f'{ID}_{TAG}_pose1.pdbqt'
    out_pdb = f'{ID}_{TAG}_pose1.pdb'

    # Extraer pose 1
    in_pose = False
    with open(vina_file) as f, open(pose_file, 'w') as out:
        for line in f:
            if line.startswith('MODEL 1'):
                in_pose = True
            elif line.startswith('ENDMDL') and in_pose:
                out.write(line)
                break
            if in_pose:
                out.write(line)

    # Convertir PDBQT a PDB (remover cargas y tipos de átomo)
    with open(pose_file) as f, open(out_pdb, 'w') as out:
        for line in f:
            if line.startswith(('ATOM', 'HETATM')):
                # Formato PDBQT: columns 1-6 record, 7-11 serial, 13-16 name, 17 altLoc,
                #                18-20 resName, 22 chainID, 23-26 resSeq, 27 iCode,
            (pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr$

                #                68-76 partialCharge, 78-79 atomType
                record = line[0:6]
                serial = line[6:11]
                name = line[12:16]
                altLoc = line[16]
                resName = line[17:20]
                chainID = line[21]
                resSeq = line[22:26]
                iCode = line[26]
                x = line[30:38]
                y = line[38:46]

# 2) Docking dirigido al centroide de los metales (caja 25 Å, exhaustiveness 16)
VINA=/home/me/miniconda3/envs/vina/bin/vina

for ID in 2Y9X 3NQ1 5I38 5M8L 4P6R; do
  CENTRO=$(python3 - "$ID" << 'PYEOF'
import sys
ID=sys.argv[1]; xs=[]; ys=[]; zs=[]
for l in open(f'{ID}_clean.pdb'):
    if l.startswith('HETATM') and l[17:20].strip() in ('CU','ZN'):
        xs.append(float(l[30:38])); ys.append(float(l[38:46])); zs.append(float(l[46:54]))
print(f"{sum(xs)/len(xs):.3f} {sum(ys)/len(ys):.3f} {sum(zs)/len(zs):.3f}")
PYEOF
)
  read CX CY CZ <<< "$CENTRO"
  for TAG in pH50 pH74; do
    if [ "$ID" = "5M8L" ] || [ "$ID" = "4P6R" ]; then
      R=${ID}_${TAG}_receptor_ZnCu.pdbqt
    else
      R=${ID}_${TAG}_receptor.pdbqt
    fi
    echo "=== DIRIGIDO $ID $TAG (centro metal: $CX $CY $CZ) ==="
    $VINA --receptor $R --ligand ../deca_alone.pdbqt \
        --center_x $CX --center_y $CY --center_z $CZ \
        --size_x 25 --size_y 25 --size_z 25 \
        --exhaustiveness 16 --num_modes 10 \
        --out vina_${ID}_${TAG}_metal.pdbqt
    grep -A 2 "mode |" vina_${ID}_${TAG}_metal.pdbqt | head -3
  done
done(pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr$

# Re-tipar Zn -> "Zn" (case-sensitive: así lo acepta Vina)
for ID in 5M8L 4P6R; do
  for TAG in pH50 pH74; do
    sed 's/ ZN$/ Zn/' ${ID}_${TAG}_receptor.pdbqt > ${ID}_${TAG}_receptor_Zn.pdbqt
  done
done

for ID in 5M8L 4P6R; do
  CENTRO=$(python3 - "$ID" << 'PYEOF'
import sys
ID=sys.argv[1]; xs=[]; ys=[]; zs=[]
for l in open(f'{ID}_clean.pdb'):
    if l.startswith('HETATM') and l[17:20].strip() in ('CU','ZN'):
        xs.append(float(l[30:38])); ys.append(float(l[38:46])); zs.append(float(l[46:54]))
print(f"{sum(xs)/len(xs):.3f} {sum(ys)/len(ys):.3f} {sum(zs)/len(zs):.3f}")
PYEOF
)
  read CX CY CZ <<< "$CENTRO"
  for TAG in pH50 pH74; do
    echo "=== DIRIGIDO $ID $TAG ==="
    $VINA --receptor ${ID}_${TAG}_receptor_Zn.pdbqt --ligand ../deca_alone.pdbqt \
        --center_x $CX --center_y $CY --center_z $CZ \
        --size_x 25 --size_y 25 --size_z 25 \
        --exhaustiveness 16 --num_modes 10 \
        --out vina_${ID}_${TAG}_metal.pdbqt 2> err.log
    if [ ! -s vina_${ID}_${TAG}_metal.pdbqt ]; then
      echo "  Zn falló; respaldo sin metales"
      $VINA --receptor ${ID}_${TAG}_receptor_noZn.pdbqt --ligand ../deca_alone.pdbqt \
          --center_x $CX --center_y $CY --center_z $CZ \
          --size_x 25 --size_y 25 --size_z 25 \
          --exhaustiveness 16 --num_modes 10 \
          --out vina_${ID}_${TAG}_metal.pdbqt
    fi
    grep -A 2 "mode |" vina_${ID}_${TAG}_metal.pdbqt | head -3
  done
done

cer_complejos.py

cat > ver_complejos_tyr.py << 'EOF'
from pymol import cmd
import glob, os
B="/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr/"
cmd.reinitialize()
for f in sorted(glob.glob(B+"*_complex.pdb")):
    nm=os.path.basename(f).replace("_complex.pdb","")
    cmd.load(f,nm)
    cmd.show("cartoon",nm); cmd.color("palecyan",nm)
    cmd.show("sticks",f"{nm} and resn DVT"); cmd.color("orange",f"{nm} and resn DVT and name V*")
    cmd.color("red",f"{nm} and resn DVT and name O*")
    cmd.show("spheres",f"{nm} and (resn CU+ZN)"); cmd.color("cyan",f"{nm} and (resn CU+ZN)")
    cmd.show("sticks",f"{nm} and (resn 0TR+KOJ+TYR)"); cmd.color("yellow",f"{nm} and (resn 0TR+KOJ+TYR)")
cmd.hide("everything","elem H")
cmd.bg_color("white")
print("listo: cada objeto = un complejo; usa 'disable' para aislar el que quieras")
EOF

# Docking ciego de híbridos (caja grande, exhaustiveness 8)
for ID in 2Y9X 3NQ1 5I38 5M8L 4P6R; do
  for TAG in pH50 pH74; do
    read CX CY CZ SX SY SZ < ${ID}_box.txt
    for HYB in hybrid_deca_1ade hybrid_deca_quercetin; do
      echo "=== $ID $TAG + $HYB ==="
      $VINA --receptor ${ID}_${TAG}_receptor.pdbqt \
           --ligand ../${HYB}.pdbqt \
           --center_x $CX --center_y $CY --center_z $CZ \
           --size_x $SX --size_y $SY --size_z $SZ \
           --exhaustiveness 8 --num_modes 10 \
           --out vina_${ID}_${TAG}_${HYB}.pdbqt
      grep -A 2 "mode |" vina_${ID}_${TAG}_${HYB}.pdbqt | head -3
    done
  done
done(pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr$ VINA=/home/me/miniconda3/envs/vina/bin/vina

# Re-lanzar híbridos para 5M8L y 4P6R usando receptores con Zn retipado
for ID in 5M8L 4P6R; do
  for TAG in pH50 pH74; do
    read CX CY CZ SX SY SZ < ${ID}_box.txt
    for HYB in hybrid_deca_1ade hybrid_deca_quercetin; do
      echo "=== $ID $TAG + $HYB (con Zn retipado) ==="
      $VINA --receptor ${ID}_${TAG}_receptor_Zn.pdbqt \
           --ligand ../${HYB}.pdbqt \
           --center_x $CX --center_y $CY --center_z $CZ \
           --size_x $SX --size_y $SY --size_z $SZ \
           --exhaustiveness 8 --num_modes 10 \
           --out vina_${ID}_${TAG}_${HYB}.pdbqt
      grep -A 2 "mode |" vina_${ID}_${TAG}_${HYB}.pdbqt | head -3
    done
  done
done(pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr$ VINA=/home/me/miniconda3/envs/vina/bin/vina
(pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr$

import sys, numpy as np
from pathlib import Path

ID, TAG, HYB = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]
CAT={'ARG','LYS','HIS'}
AA={'ALA','ARG','ASN','ASP','CYS','GLN','GLU','GLY','HIS','ILE','LEU','LYS',
    'MET','PHE','PRO','SER','THR','TRP','TYR','VAL'}

pose=[]; inm=False
pfile = Path(f'vina_{ID}_{TAG}_{HYB}.pdbqt')
if not pfile.exists():
    print(f"⚠️  No existe {pfile}"); sys.exit(0)

for l in open(pfile):
    if l.startswith('MODEL 1'): inm=True; continue
    if l.startswith('ENDMDL') and inm: break
    if inm and l.startswith(('ATOM','HETATM')):
        pose.append([float(l[30:38]),float(l[38:46]),float(l[46:54])])
if not pose:
    print(f"⚠️  {pfile} sin pose 1"); sys.exit(0)
V=np.array(pose)

prot=[]
for l in open(f'{ID}_clean.pdb'):
    if l.startswith(('ATOM','HETATM')):
        prot.append((l[21],l[22:26].strip(),l[17:20].strip(),l[12:16].strip(),
                     [float(l[30:38]),float(l[38:46]),float(l[46:54])]))

hit={}; cu=[]; lig=[]
for ch,rs,rn,nm,xyz in prot:
    d=float(np.linalg.norm(V-np.array(xyz),axis=1).min())
    if rn in ('CU','ZN'): cu.append((rn,ch,rs,d))
    elif rn not in AA and rn!='HOH': lig.append((rn,ch,rs,d))
    if d<5.0 and rn in AA:
        k=(ch,rs,rn); hit[k]=min(hit.get(k,99),d)

ncat=sum(1 for k in hit if k[2] in CAT)
pct_cat=100*ncat/max(1,len(hit))
allres={(ch,rs):rn for ch,rs,rn,nm,xyz in prot if rn in AA}
nb=sum(1 for rn in allres.values() if rn in CAT)
baseline=100*nb/len(allres)
enrich=pct_cat/baseline if baseline>0 else 0

(pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr$
dcu=min([t[3] for t in cu]) if cu else 999
dlig=min([t[3] for t in lig]) if lig else 999

print(f"=== {ID} {TAG} + {HYB} pose 1 | atomos: {len(V)} ===")
print(f"  Contactos: {len(hit)} | Cat%: {pct_cat:.1f}% | Baseline: {baseline:.1f}% | Enriquecimiento: {enrich:.2f}x")
print(f"  dCu/Zn: {dcu:.2f} Å | dLig: {dlig:.2f} Å")
print(f"  Top3 residuos: ", end="")
for k,d in sorted(hit.items(),key=lambda kv:kv[1])[:3]:
    print(f"{k[0(pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set/tyr$ cat > anal_hibridos.py << 'EOF'

>         prot.append((l[21],l[22:26].strip(),l[17:20].strip(),l[12:16].strip(),
>                      [float(l[30:38]),float(l[38:46]),float(l[46:54])]))
>
> hit={}; cu=[]; lig=[]
> for ch,rs,rn,nm,xyz in prot:
>     d=float(np.linalg.norm(V-np.array(xyz),axis=1).min())
>     if rn in ('CU','ZN'): cu.append((rn,ch,rs,d))
>     elif rn not in AA and rn!='HOH': lig.append((rn,ch,rs,d))
>     if d<5.0 and rn in AA:
>         k=(ch,rs,rn); hit[k]=min(hit.get(k,99),d)
>
> ncat=sum(1 for k in hit if k[2] in CAT)
> pct_cat=100*ncat/max(1,len(hit))
> allres={(ch,rs):rn for ch,rs,rn,nm,xyz in prot if rn in AA}
> nb=sum(1 for rn in allres.values() if rn in CAT)
> baseline=100*nb/len(allres)
> enrich=pct_cat/baseline if baseline>0 else 0
>
> dcu=min([t[3] for t in cu]) if cu else 999
> dlig=min([t[3] for t in lig]) if lig else 999
>
> print(f"=== {ID} {TAG} + {HYB} pose 1 | atomos: {len(V)} ===")
> print(f"  Contactos: {len(hit)} | Cat%: {pct_cat:.1f}% | Baseline: {baseline:.1f}% | Enriquecimiento: {enrich:.2f}x")
> print(f"  dCu/Zn: {dcu:.2f} Å | dLig: {dlig:.2f} Å")
> print(f"  Top3 residuos: ", end="")
> for k,d in sorted(hit.items(),key=lambda kv:kv[1])[:3]:
>     print(f"{k[0]}{k[1]}{k[2]} ", end="")
> print()
> EOF
d_deca_quercetin']:
        f = f'vina_{ID}_{TAG}_{suffix}.pdbqt'
        if Path(f).exists():
            with open(f) as fh:
                for line in fh:
                    if re.match(r'\s+1\s+', line):
                        vals[suffix] = float(line.split()[1])
                        break
    deca = vals.get('deca', None)
    ade = vals.get('hybrid_deca_1ade', None)
    que = vals.get('hybrid_deca_quercetin', None)
    d_ade = f"{ade-deca:+.2f}" if (deca and ade) else "N/A"
    d_que = f"{que-deca:+.2f}" if (deca and que) else "N/A"
    def fmt(v): return f"{v:.2f}" if v else "N/A"
    print(f"{ID:<6} {TAG:<6} {fmt(deca):<12} {fmt(ade):<12} {fmt(que):<12} {d_ade:<8} {d_que:<8}")

print("=" * 100)
